        <script>
        function createCookie(name, value, expires) {
            document.cookie = name + "=" + value + "; max-age=" + expires + "; path=/";
        }
        function checkCookie(name) {
            const nameEQ = name + "=";
            const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
            for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i = i + 1) {
                let c = ca[i];
                while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') c = c.substring(1, c.length);
                if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
            }
            return null;
        }
        // Initialize consent mode
        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        function gtag() { dataLayer.push(arguments); }

        // Check if user has already made a consent choice
        const consentCookie = checkCookie('user_consent');

        if (consentCookie) {
            // User has previously consented - parse their choices
            const consent = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(consentCookie));
            gtag('consent', 'default', consent);
        } else {
            // First visit - set default to denied
            gtag('consent', 'default', {
                'ad_user_data': 'denied',
                'ad_personalization': 'denied',
                'ad_storage': 'denied',
                'analytics_storage': 'denied',
                'wait_for_update': 500
            });
        }
    </script>

    <h2 class="editorH2"><strong>Premier League 2026/27 Winner Odds</strong></h2>

<p>There are a number of factors you should consider when selecting the outright winner of the English Premier League: new signings, new managers, recent season performances, and the ability to maintain a consistent level of excellence throughout a long and gruelling campaign. Try and think objectively when looking at Premier League title odds; as football fans we love emotion, but this doesn&rsquo;t always pay dividends on a bet slip!</p>

<p>Arsenal were crowned Premier League champions in 2025/26 ahead of Manchester City, ending a 22-year wait for the trophy. The Gunners have been installed as the early favourites to retain the title in 2026/27, with the likes of Man City, Man United, and Liverpool also in contention according to the latest odds.</p>

</div>

<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2">Who will win the 2026/27 Premier League?</h2>

<p>Below, we take a closer look at the leading contenders for the 2026/27 Premier League title and their chances of lifting the trophy.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Arsenal</h3>

<p>Arsenal head into the 2026/27 season as the early favourites in the Premier League Winner market. Mikel Arteta's side ended years of near-misses in 2025/26 when they finally got their hands on the trophy for the first time since 2004. The Gunners have finished in the top-2 in each of the last four seasons and look well placed to mount another charge for the title.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Manchester City</h3>

<p>Manchester City enter into a new era following the exit of Pep Guardiola, who has been replaced by former Chelsea manager Enzo Maresca. The Italian inherits a strong side that will be hungry to bounce back from consecutive campaigns without a title.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Liverpool</h3>

<p>Liverpool are expected to be firmly in the title conversation during the 2026/27 campaign, with their attacking quality and high-intensity style making them one of the division's most dangerous teams. The Reds have also changed managers, replacing title-winner Arne Slot with Andoni Iraola from Bournemouth. He will now be tasked with getting a tune out of the likes of Alexander Isak and Florian Wirtz.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Manchester United</h3>

<p>Manchester United enter the season looking to re-establish themselves among the Premier League's elite after periods of inconsistency in recent campaigns. With a talented squad and one of the biggest global fanbases in football, expectations remain high at Old Trafford, with Michael Carrick now in as permanent manager.</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Chelsea</h3>

<p>Chelsea continue to be viewed as a team with significant potential thanks to their investment in young talent and long-term squad development. The Blues have shown signs of improvement and possess the attacking options to compete with the league's leading sides. If Xabi Alonso's young squad can develop consistency and adapt to the pressure of a title race, Chelsea could prove to be an interesting contender in the 2026/27 Premier League Winner market.</p>

</div>
<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2"><strong>Premier League Winner Betting Markets</strong></h2>

<p>Finding the most appealing odds on the Premier League outright winner market is the most popular form of bet in this area, but it&rsquo;s not your only option. Here are some of the other ways you can use Premier League title odds.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Outright Winner</strong></h3>

<p>The simplest bet here is the Premier League outright winner. Make your selection as close to the start of the season as possible to find the best odds. Leicester City were priced at 5000/1 at the beginning of their title win in 15/16.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Each-Way</strong></h3>

<p>Premier League betting sites will take each-way bets on champions, meaning your selection finishing second, third and maybe even fourth will be enough for a payout. Of course, odds will be shorter &ndash; particularly if your selection finishes in the lowest each-way place.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Premier League Winner Without (Club)</strong></h3>

<p>Selecting this market means ruling out one club in particular for the title. With Man City&rsquo;s dominance in recent years, it really only makes sense to nominate them as the club you wish to exclude when wagering in this market. In this scenario, you&rsquo;ll receive a payout if any team other than City win the league.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Straight Forecast</strong></h3>

<p>To win a straight forecast bet, you&rsquo;ll need to pick the teams finishing first and second in the top flight in the correct order. You can increase the number of positions in the forecast to increase the odds, but the teams must finish in exactly the position you picked to win the bet.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3">Winner/Top Goalscorer</h3>

<p>Similarly with straight forecast wagers, the winner/top goalscorer market is like two selections rolled into the same bet. Here, you&rsquo;re selecting both the Premier League champions and the season&rsquo;s top goalscorer. Your golden boot pick doesn&rsquo;t have to come from the team you&rsquo;ve backed to win the league; for example, you could bet on Arsenal and Haaland here.&nbsp;</p>

<h3 class="editorH3"><strong>Trophy Multiples</strong></h3>

<p>Think that City will be able to repeat their treble success? You can lengthen your odds by betting on them to do it again.</p>

</div>
<div class="text-block post-block link-underline ">
    <h2 class="editorH2"><strong>Premier League Winner Betting Tips</strong></h2>

<p>BettingOdds will provide expert betting tips throughout the Premier League season, so it&rsquo;s worth checking back regularly to gain insight on upcoming matches. Take a look at the points below on how to make use of our site.</p>

<ul>
	<li><strong>Read Our Articles &mdash; </strong>We&rsquo;ll bring you detailed analysis of upcoming fixtures, team news and recent form so that you can make informed bets on Premier League winners and other similar markets.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Compare Odds &mdash; </strong>Premier League odds will fluctuate throughout the season but you can use our tools to easily compare the best odds from all the top bookmakers.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Research &mdash;</strong> It&rsquo;s vital that you conduct some of your own research if you&rsquo;re to improve your chances of converting bets into winnings. Take a look at recent form, statistics, injury updates, and fixture lists to gain valuable insight &ndash; all of this and more will be available at BettingOdds during the season.&nbsp;</li>
	<li><strong>Know Your Limits &mdash; </strong>Betting on the Premier League is an activity enjoyed by millions of fans across the world, but it&rsquo;s important to know your limits and only bet what you&rsquo;re happy to lose. Set budgets, never bet when under the influence of drugs or alcohol and don&rsquo;t chase your losses.&nbsp;</li>
</ul>
